# Silver certificate deformation: source reconstruction and three viability screens

Status: JOINT UNVERIFIED ANALYTICAL DRAFT. No formal submission, accepted new
fact, full-class bound for the perturbed word, or resolution of the original
minimax target is claimed. This is max4's one allocated round after completed64,
with completed ceiling65 / attempted ceiling66. TASK.md and guidance
8fd9094550caff70 govern; plan cbe64562a9a92f5d records the work. Latest
guidance56f0c49630564f24 was read and explicitly leaves this assignment unchanged.

The outcome is three exact, narrowly scoped failed screens. The second screen
allows arbitrary terminal-gradient coefficients; the third changes the two
right-child weights independently and passes the first two levels. Neither
survives the original-coordinate test at the third level. The source itself
does give a sharp GD objective certificate for ORIGINAL Silver, but its missing
extension is precisely a primitive/history inequality already present in the
project. No new theorem for the perturbed word follows merely from retrieval.

## 1. Model, accepted inputs, and unchanged physical word

Use fact1252b8823bb5a290's normalized convention: L=1,
x_star=g_star=f_star=0, x_i=x_0-sum_(t<i) h_t g_t, and

    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2 <=0.

All formal vectors (x_0,g_0,...,g_n) remain independent when testing a
certificate. For a nonnegative physical word h, W_n(h) is the supremum of
2(f(x_n)-f(x_star))/(L||x_0-x_star||^2) over the entire smooth-convex class
and every finite dimension. Thus W=2R in the project's notation. Every ordered
row exists; unspecified multipliers below are zero, not deleted constraints.

The other accepted inputs are fact04219554e41d9750, Sections10--11
(primitive and objective identities, including their full-coordinate scope),
and fact5fd396eba75651e7, Section10 (the original benchmark comparison only).
No unverified previous obstruction or numerical optimizer is a premise.

Fix one enclosing integer m and set

    lambda=1+sqrt(2), epsilon=1/(4m), beta=1-epsilon,
    c=lambda(1+epsilon), a=beta/c,
    H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+d_j,H_j), d_j=a c^j, 0<=j<m,
    S_j=sum H_j, A_j=1+S_j, n_m=2^m-1.

Every child uses this SAME epsilon, beta, c, a. In particular testing H_3
below means the actual initial seven-step subword of H_m, not resetting m=3.
The terminal gradient is a formal sample, not another physical step.

## 2. Complete source and hypothesis mapping

### 2.1 Altschuler--Parrilo and the original full interpolation certificate

Source: Jason M. Altschuler and Pablo A. Parrilo, *Acceleration by Stepsize
Hedging II: Silver Stepsize Schedule for Smooth Convex Optimization*,
arXiv:2309.16530v1, Theorem1.1, Definition2.1, Theorem3.4, Lemmas3.5--3.7,
and AppendixA. The exact downloaded HTML reports v1, dated28September2023.
The proof, including the corrections, signs, and AppendixA expansions, was read.
URL: https://arxiv.org/html/2309.16530 .

The source's nonnegative residual is

    P_ij=2(f_i-f_j)+2<g_j,x_j-x_i>-||g_i-g_j||^2 = -2 Q_ji.

Consequently both a transpose and a factor2 are required when transferring
its multipliers. Its theorem is for a convex L-smooth function with a chosen
minimizer, arbitrary initialization and dimension, ORIGINAL Silver word
H^0_(j+1)=(H^0_j,1+lambda^(j-1),H^0_j), and n=2^j-1.
Write A=lambda^j, S=A-1, and

    C_AP=(1+sqrt(4A^2-3))/2, r_AP=1/(2C_AP).

The source identity is

    sum sigma_ij P_ij
      =||x_0||^2-||x_n-C_AP g_n||^2-2C_AP f_n.       (2.1)

It gives W_n(H^0_j)<=1/C_AP, not r_AP. This is a direct GD objective
bound, not a proximal bound. Its multiplier recursion glues the left copy
with weight1 and the right copy with weight lambda^2=1+2lambda, adds
lambda times each actual right step in the two endpoint-to-right rows,
and corrects the three endpoint/optimum incidences. The final optimum
incidences are sigma_(star,i)=h_i for i<n, sigma_(star,n)=C_AP,
sigma_(n,star)=S. Negative correction entries must be aggregated with their
inherited entries; the final multipliers are nonnegative.

Here is a direct reconstruction in the ORIGINAL Q convention, using only
the accepted primitive identity, which also checks the source's full array.
For any word h of sum S and terminal n define

    V_h=2 sum_(i<n) h_i(f_i-f_n)
          +sum_(i<n) h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2-S(S+1)||g_n||^2.

On the original Silver word define the finite multiplier array Lambda^0
from the empty array as follows. For a join, p is the bridge index, z is the
parent terminal, and j ranges over the nonterminal right-child queries.
Let A=1+sum(child), d=A/lambda and let h_j denote its right steps. Embed
both complete child arrays and add

    Lambda^0_parent = Lambda^0_left + lambda^2 Lambda^0_right
       +2lambda [ sum_j h_j (Q_(j,p)+Q_(j,z))
                                      +Q_(z,p)+d Q_(p,z) ].   (2.2)

Here and below a sum of Q terms denotes its coefficient array. All four
added coefficient types are nonnegative. Fact04219554e41d9750, identity(5),
applies because d(2A+d)=A^2. It yields the polynomial identity

                    V_h + sum Lambda^0_ij Q_ij = 0.           (2.3)

The empty identity is0=0. The actual parent capacity is2A+d=lambda A;
induction proves A=lambda^j, so this is exactly the original physical Silver
word at every level. It is an identity on all formal gradients, with zero
PSD remainder, not a restriction to actual or coincident gradients.

To recover (2.1), take sigma_ij=Lambda^0_ji/2 on finite pairs and the
three optimum-incidence types displayed above. The finite part of the
source's Theta+Xi+Delta recursion is exactly (2.2) after this transpose and
factor2; its endpoint coefficients are 2lambda and 2lambda d in Q convention.
The remaining identity follows from

    V_h-2 sum_(i<n) h_i Q_(i,star)
      =||x_0||^2-||x_n||^2-2S f_n-S(S+1)||g_n||^2,             (2.4)
    C_AP^2-C_AP=S(S+2).

Indeed adding -2C_AP Q_(n,star)-2S Q_(star,n) to (2.4) gives the
right-hand side of (2.1). This checks all coefficients without invoking
the source's computer-algebra script. In project normalization the full
PSD identity is the square ||x_n-C_AP g_n||^2/C_AP, with finite multipliers
Lambda^0/C_AP, prefix-to-optimum weights2h_i/C_AP, terminal-to-optimum
weight2, and optimum-to-terminal weight2S/C_AP.

The SAME finite baseline array has the sharper completion

    ||x_0||^2-2D f_n+sum Lambda^0_ij Q_ij
       +2 sum_(i<n) h_i Q_(i,star)+2A Q_(n,star)
                         =||x_n-A g_n||^2,  D=2S+1=2A-1.    (2.5)

For an explicit expansion, substitute (2.3) and (2.4), then use
A^2-A=S(S+1) and D=S+A. Thus every coefficient in (2.5) is nonnegative,
and division by D gives a full original-space objective certificate:

                         W_n(H^0_j)<=1/(2lambda^j-1).        (2.6)

The matching Huber function has slope t=1/D, value x^2/2 for |x|<=t,
and t|x|-t^2/2 otherwise. Start at1, with minimizer0. Since every step is
nonnegative and each prefix sum is at most S, every query lies in the
positive affine piece: x_i=1-t sum_(k<i) h_k and x_n=(S+1)/D>=t.
Its value satisfies2f(x_n)=1/D. This proves tightness of the baseline
completion within this joint reconstruction. It supplies no perturbed-word
certificate. No separate unverified intermediate result is being adopted.

### 2.2 Chikhi: product identity, joint potential, and proximal output

Source: Salah R. Chikhi, *Last-Iterate Performance of Gradient Descent and
Relaxed Proximal Point via s-Composability*, arXiv:2609.13765v1,
Definition1, Proposition1 with proof, Theorem1 with equality construction,
Lemma2 with proof, and Section5/Theorem4 with proof.
URL: https://arxiv.org/html/2609.13765v1 .

Its definition requires a positive schedule and BOTH

    eta=1/(1+S)=product_(i<n)(h_i-1),
    ||x_0-x_star||^2 >= ||x_n-x_star||^2
                    +S(S+1)||g_n||^2+2S(f_n-f_star).          (2.7)

The source obtains Silver's s-composability from Grimmer et al., Lemma6.
No uninspected theorem of that paper is needed here: (2.3)--(2.4) reconstruct
the necessary baseline inequality from the accepted project identity.
For a varying schedule the history expression in Chikhi's Lemma2 is

    sum h_i[2(f_i-f_n)+||g_i||^2+2<g_i,x_0-x_i>]
           -||x_n-x_0||^2-S(S+1)||g_n||^2.

Expanding x_0-x_i=sum_(t<i)h_t g_t cancels every off-diagonal product
against the squared displacement, leaving exactly V_h. Adding the
nonnegative source residuals sum h_i P_(star,i) means subtracting
2sum h_i Q_(i,star), which gives (2.7) by (2.4). Thus an appeal to this
history mechanism for H_m is precisely the already-unpaid primitive
inequality; the new paper does not furnish it for a perturbed word.

For a proper closed convex F with an attained minimum and mu>0, the
source's relaxed proximal iteration is

    z_i=prox_(mu F)(x_i), x_(i+1)=x_i+h_i(z_i-x_i),
    z_n=prox_(mu F)(x_n).

Its exact theorem is sup mu(F(z_n)-F_star)/||x_0-x_star||^2=1/[4(S+1)]
for a positive s-composable schedule. The equality example is F(x)=t|x|
with t=R/[2mu(S+1)], x_0=R, and z_n=R/2. For original Silver this is
1/(4lambda^j). Its terminal point is z_n, not our x_n.

The normalized envelope f=mu F_mu satisfies g_n=x_n-z_n and

                   f(x_n)-f_star=mu(F(z_n)-F_star)+||g_n||^2/2.

Dropping the last term would misidentify the objective. The source's joint
terminal inequality instead gives, with t=||g_n||,

    mu(F(z_n)-F_star)+(S+1)t^2<=R t,
    f(x_n)-f_star <= R t-(S+1/2)t^2 <= R^2/[2(2S+1)].        (2.8)

This is the paid conversion, with W<=1/(2S+1). For an arbitrary smooth
f, the same result follows directly from (2.7) and the original optimum
interpolation inequality; no assumption that a particular smooth f has
already been supplied as an envelope is necessary. Formula(2.5) gives its
explicit original-row certificate whenever (2.3) is available.

The perturbed H_m does not satisfy the definition's product identity.
Set v=a/(c-2), so0<v<1. The physical sum identity, checked by substitution
in A_(j+1)=2A_j+d_j, is

    A_j=v c^j+(1-v)2^j.

Counting each level's multiplicity, or inducting in the physical word, gives

    product_(i<n_m)(h_i-1)=beta^(2^m-1)c^(-m),
    A_m product_(i<n_m)(h_i-1)
      =beta^(2^m-1)[v+(1-v)(2/c)^m] <1.                      (2.9)

This prevents using the named s-composability theorem as stated. It does
NOT disprove the joint potential without the product identity.

### 2.3 Wang--Ma--Yang--Zhou: the older metrics

Source: Bofan Wang, Shiqian Ma, Junfeng Yang and Danqing Zhou,
*Relaxed Proximal Point Algorithm: Tight Complexity Bounds and Acceleration
without Momentum*, arXiv:2410.08890, Lemma5.1 and Theorems5.2--5.3 with the
displayed proofs. URL: https://arxiv.org/html/2410.08890 .

For arbitrary convex L-smooth h and original Silver, Theorem5.2 states the
tight GD objective bound (h(x_n)-h_star)/(L R^2)<=1/(4lambda^j-2), and
gradient bound ||grad h(x_n)||/(L R)<=lambda^(-j). Its finite array in
Lemma5.1 is Lambda^0 transposed and divided by2: its residual is -Q_ji
and its identity(34) has right-hand side V_h/2. The weighted optimum completion
is reproduced explicitly in (2.5); no typography in the extracted proof
is being used as an algebraic premise.

Theorem5.3 applies this to an envelope and bounds
F(z_n)-F_star+(mu/2)||grad F_mu(x_n)||^2 by R^2/[mu(4lambda^j-2)].
The discussion calls the proximal objective bound obtained by dropping
the residual term non-tight. That is the older gap addressed by Chikhi.
It is not a new sharper bound for the GD objective of a perturbed word.

## 3. A paid terminal-coefficient relaxation

For a word of sum S define the generalized history form

    V_(h,K)=2 sum h_i(f_i-f_n)+sum h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2-K||g_n||^2.

Suppose a proposed nonnegative finite array has the exact full identity
V_(h,K)+sum Lambda_ij Q_ij=M(g), where M is a quadratic form in ALL
formal gradients. This equation defines M; it does not presume PSD.
For K>=0 put

    b_K=(1+sqrt(1+4K))/2, F_K=S+b_K.

Expanding (2.4) with K in place of S(S+1) gives

    ||x_0||^2-2F_K f_n+sum Lambda_ij Q_ij
          +2sum h_i Q_(i,star)+2b_K Q_(n,star)
                          =M(g)+||x_n-b_K g_n||^2.           (3.1)

All added coefficients are nonnegative. If M>=0, division by F_K would give

    W_n(h)<=1/F_K=2/[2S+1+sqrt(1+4K)].

Relative to Huber1/(2S+1), the loss is exactly

    chi_K=(2S+1)/(S+b_K).                                   (3.2)

If only a radius-inflated joint inequality with constant r_rad were proved,
the corresponding upper would pay the additional factor r_rad. Neither a
smaller K nor a different radius coefficient is free. The screens below test
the actual M, not a presumed child oracle or an informal potential estimate.

## 4. One full-coordinate coefficient expansion for the screens

Consider (h,1+d,h), with child sum s, A=s+1, T=2A+d, parent sum T-1.
Let p denote its bridge query and z its terminal. Let r_vec=sum_j h_j g_j
over the nonterminal right child. Its two copies of V_(h,K) have factor1
and kappa. Introduce the nonnegative correction array

    E=u sum_j h_j Q_(j,p)+v_w sum_j h_j Q_(j,z)
                                      +p_w Q_(z,p)+z_w Q_(p,z).

The symbol v_w is a multiplier weight, distinct from v=a/(c-2).
For a nonempty right child, all function values cancel in
V_(parent,K_+)-V_left-kappa V_right+E iff

    u+v_w=2(kappa-1),
    u s+p_w-z_w=2(A+d).                                     (4.1)

When the right child is empty, choosing these identities remains a valid
definition of the continuation. Empty sums are zero; no formal endpoint is
identified with a different endpoint. The resulting original quadratic is

    M_def_parent=M_def_left+kappa M_def_right+R,
    R=(u-v_w)||r_vec||^2/2
       +(u d-z_w)<r_vec,g_p>+(p_w-v_w)<r_vec,g_z>
       +(p_w d-z_w)<g_p,g_z>+C_p||g_p||^2+C_z||g_z||^2,       (4.2)
    C_p=K-d-d^2+(u s+p_w)/2-(d+1/2)z_w,
    C_z=-K_++kappa K+v_w s/2+(p_w+z_w)/2.

For a check that this retains remote and mixed terms, the gradient parts of
the four sums in E, before multiplication by u,v_w,p_w,z_w, are

    sum h_j Q_(j,p): d<r_vec,g_p>+||r_vec||^2/2
                       +sum h_j(1-h_j)||g_j||^2/2+s||g_p||^2/2;
    sum h_j Q_(j,z): -||r_vec||^2/2
                       +sum h_j(1-h_j)||g_j||^2/2
                       +s||g_z||^2/2-<r_vec,g_z>;
    Q_(z,p): d<g_z,g_p>+<g_z,r_vec>+(||g_p||^2+||g_z||^2)/2;
    Q_(p,z): -(d+1/2)||g_p||^2+||g_z||^2/2
                                       -<g_p,r_vec>-<g_p,g_z>.

Their value parts immediately give (4.1). Adding the history diagonals gives
(4.2). Thus this is an identity on the original space, with no assumed
coincidence of gradients and no quotient-space PSD test.

## 5. The first actual levels already detect the deformation

For every enclosing m>=3, c<3, so0<d_j<A_j at every level. To see this,
A_j>v c^j=d_j/(c-2) and c-2<1. Also

    t_j=d_j/A_j=(c-2)/[1+(1/v-1)(2/c)^j]

is strictly increasing in j. Put t_star=1/lambda. At the first three levels,

    d_0=a, A_0=1; d_1=beta, A_1=2+a;
    d_2=beta c, A_2=4+a(2+c),
    t_0<t_star, t_1<t_star, t_2>t_star.                      (5.1)

The first inequality follows from a<1/lambda. For the second,

    lambda d_1-A_1=epsilon[-lambda+2/(lambda(1+epsilon))]<0.

For the third, use lambda^3=5lambda+2 and expand only the displayed scalar:

    c(lambda d_2-A_2)
      =epsilon[(lambda+4)-(4lambda+2)epsilon
                                      -(5lambda+2)epsilon^2] >0.       (5.2)

The bracket is positive already for0<epsilon<=1/4: use2<lambda<3 to
bound it below by6-14/4-17/16>0. No schedule recurrence or numerical
evaluation was executed to obtain these textual identities.

## 6. Screen1: equal-weight source transport, canonical terminal coefficient

This law preserves the source's four coefficient ratios, substitutes the
actual A,d, and fixes the scalar gluing factor by exact value flow:

    kappa=(3A+d)/(A-d), k=kappa-1=2(A+d)/(A-d),
    u=v_w=p_w=k, z_w=k d,
    K=A(A-1), K_+=T(T-1).                                  (6.1)

All coefficients are nonnegative. At original Silver, d=A/lambda and
kappa=lambda^2, so this is exactly (2.2), not an unrelated seed.
Substitution in (4.2) leaves

    R=Delta(||g_z||^2-||g_p||^2),
    Delta=kappa A^2-T^2=kappa d^2-A^2,
    Delta/A^2=(1+t)(t^2+2t-1)/(1-t), t=d/A.                (6.2)

At the actual first step t=t_0<t_star, Delta<0. The empty child remainders
are zero, so the formal direction g_0=0,g_1=1 gives M_def_1=Delta<0.
The canonical recursive child certificate therefore fails at its first
perturbed step. Its value-flow and signs alone are not a certificate.
Stop this screen here; Screen2 tests the substantive terminal repair.

## 7. Screen2: arbitrary terminal-gradient coefficients do not repair this law

Retain the finite multiplier recursion(6.1), but now allow ANY real
K_0=0,K_1,...,K_m, consistently in every same-depth child. Use K_j in the
child and K_(j+1) in the parent history form. This includes every weakening
of the canonical terminal coefficient, with its objective cost paid by(3.2)
when K_m>=0. No feasibility of an intermediate child is assumed below.

The actual full M_def_j remains diagonal: its child matrices are diagonal and
(4.2) becomes

    R=(K_j+A_j-kappa_j d_j^2)||g_p||^2
       +[-K_(j+1)+kappa_j K_j
                       +(kappa_j-1)(A_j+d_j)/2]||g_z||^2.   (7.1)

Let T_j^end be the actual terminal diagonal of M_def_j and set
E_def_j=T_j^end+K_j, D_def_j=E_def_j-A_j(A_j-1). These are scalar coefficient
identities, not executed recurrences. From(7.1),

    E_def_0=D_def_0=0,
    E_def_(j+1)=kappa_j E_def_j+(kappa_j-1)(A_j+d_j)/2,
    D_def_(j+1)=kappa_j D_def_j+Delta_j,
    actual bridge diagonal = E_def_j+A_j-kappa_j d_j^2
                            =D_def_j-Delta_j.                  (7.2)

Every K_j has canceled from the bridge diagonal. By(5.1) and(6.2),
Delta_0<0, Delta_1<0, Delta_2>0. Therefore

    D_def_2=kappa_1 Delta_0+Delta_1<0,
    (M_def_3)_(3,3)=D_def_2-Delta_2<0.                              (7.3)

This is the ORIGINAL coordinate g_3, the physical bridge of H_3. Thus the
free-terminal-coefficient repair fails on an elementary principal diagonal;
it is not merely a negative modular remainder that a child might absorb.
For orientation, at the first step K_1=E_def_1 makes the terminal diagonal zero
and leaves the other diagonal positive, so the repair really passes that
first failure before encountering(7.3).

Even inflating only the final radius/objective level cannot repair the fixed
full array at the target horizons. Already in H_4 the two H_3 copies have
negative diagonals at g_3 and g_11, equal to a_neg and kappa_3 a_neg,
where a_neg=D_def_2-Delta_2<0. Choose g_3=1,g_11=-1 and every other gradient
zero. Their actual physical steps both equal1+d_2, so the displacement
sum h_i g_i is zero. With x_0=0, g_n=0, the finishing square in(3.1) is
zero for ANY terminal coefficient b_K, whereas M=(1+kappa_3)a_neg<0.
All later enclosing joins leave this initial H_4 direction untouched.
Adding any multiple of ||x_0||^2 also vanishes on it. This is only an
obstruction to the fixed array(6.1), not to a reoptimized full certificate.

## 8. Screen3: unequal right weights preserving the primitive null vector

Return to K=A(A-1), K_+=T(T-1), but permit the two right weights to differ.
This repair changes the actual finite array. A concrete source-calibrated law is

    u=2lambda,
    v_w=[u d+2(A+d)]/A,
    kappa=1+(u+v_w)/2,
    z_w=2A, p_w=4A+2d-u(A-1)
                  =2lambda+2(d-A/lambda).                  (8.1)

It satisfies both value-flow equations(4.1), and at d=A/lambda recovers
u=v_w=p_w=2lambda, z_w=2A and kappa=lambda^2: exactly the source.
All actual coefficients are nonnegative at EVERY level. The weights
u,v_w,z_w,kappa are positive. At j=0, p_w=4+2d_0>0; at j=1,
A_1<lambda implies p_w>2lambda-2>0; for j>=2, monotonicity in(5.1)
gives d_j>A_j/lambda and hence p_w>2lambda.

The full canonical primitive form vanishes on common gradients: choose
all g_i equal and choose the affine-consistent f_i, so all finite Q's and
V_h are zero. Thus every PSD primitive matrix has the all-ones vector e
in its kernel. In(8.1), direct substitution in(4.2) preserves M_def_j e=0
algebraically at every level, even before asserting PSD.

More explicitly, set delta=t_star-d/A, Y=r_vec-s g_z and Z=g_p-g_z.
The entire correction is

    R=delta[(lambda+1)||Y||^2-2lambda A<Y,Z>
                                +A^2(2+t_star+d/A)||Z||^2].  (8.2)

The bracket is positive definite in Y,Z: its leading diagonal is positive
and its determinant divided by A^2 is

    (lambda+1)(2+t_star+d/A)-lambda^2 >= lambda>0.

Thus(5.1) proves PSD of M_def_1 and M_def_2 by the actual embedded child sums.
Both their signs and their full original remainders pass. At level j=2,
choose ALL left-child gradients, including g_p, equal to1, and ALL
right-child gradients, including g_z, equal to0. The left M_def_2 vanishes on
its common-gradient kernel; the right term vanishes on zero. Formula(4.2)
or(8.2) gives the full original value

                 M_def_3=A_2^2-2A_2 d_2-d_2^2<0.               (8.3)

The vector uses g_0=g_1=g_2=g_3=1 and g_4=...=g_7=0. It is a
formal PSD test, not an asserted interpolable primal trajectory. No
gradient equality was imposed in the certificate's domain.

The failure is not a poor choice of the remaining scalar u. In the more
general four-correction template(4.1), suppose the canonical children are
PSD. Full parent PSD would require M_def_parent e=0. The bridge row of R e is
(A+d)(2A-z_w)/2, so z_w=2A. The right aggregate row requires
u d-v_w A+2(A+d)=0. On the same left-common/right-zero vector the full
value is then A^2-2Ad-d^2, independently of u,v_w,kappa,p_w. Thus no
scalar choice in this Huber-kernel-preserving template repairs this level.
This is a short original-coordinate test, not an all-depth impossibility
argument for unrestricted primitive or objective certificates.

For completeness, merely changing the objective level of this fixed full
array also cannot help. Embed the vector w from(8.3) in the first H_3 of
H_m, setting all later coordinates to zero. Every enclosing correction
vanishes on w, because the enclosing bridge and entire right block vanish;
the negative value is unchanged. Its final gradient is zero. The whole
matrix still has M_def_m e=0. Write D=2S_m+1 and
theta=(sum_(i<n_m)h_i w_i)/D. Set g=w-theta e and x_0=0. Then
M_def_m(g)=M_def_m(w)<0 and x_n-A_m g_n=0. The completion(2.5), divided by D,
is negative in this original direction, and any extra coefficient on
||x_0||^2 vanishes. Changing the full multipliers or the potential shape
is outside this screened law.

## 9. Precisely unpaid interface and benchmark loss

The accepted benchmark is the ORIGINAL all-split recurrence of
fact04219554e41d9750, with U_n^ZJ=1/B_n. Fact5fd396eba75651e7 gives

           B_(2^m-1)/(1+2S_m)<237/250,  m>=1536, tau=1/4.   (9.1)

It is a comparison with the Huber LOWER example; it is not a full upper.
A new full certificate with W_(2^m-1)(H_m)<=chi/(1+2S_m), uniformly with
some fixed chi<250/237, would give the fixed relative improvement
1-237chi/250 on this true unbounded sequence. If the new certificate used
the valid generalized-history route(3.1), its exact required inequality is

    V_(H_m,K_m)+sum Lambda^m_ij Q_ij=M_def_m>=0
    on EVERY formal gradient, with every Lambda^m_ij>=0,
    (2S_m+1)/(S_m+(1+sqrt(1+4K_m))/2)<=chi<250/237.           (9.2)

Neither(9.2) nor a more general objective certificate was obtained. A
radius loss must additionally be multiplied into chi. Proving(9.2) is the
substantive all-function inequality, not a technical continuity estimate.

These screens show exactly what the selected source transport cannot pay:
understeps at the first levels create child slack, but the selected endpoint
and right-aggregate corrections leave an explicit original negative direction
when the next bridge exceeds A/lambda. A future continuation must change
coefficients that act on that direction, for example by changing inherited
finite arrays in their enclosing context or by using a genuinely different
full objective completion. Such a change must prove all signs and the entire
PSD remainder; it cannot invoke the primitive history inequality as a premise.

This does not exclude unrestricted nonnegative multipliers, other source
deformations, collective corrections involving left-interior coordinates,
nonprimitive potentials, or a full-class Huber-like upper for H_m. It is not
an actual lower witness against H_m. The original minimax target remains OPEN.

## 10. Retrieval, records, and resources

Primary PDF and HTML files and their extracted source text are under downloads/.
The source text reader is extract_source_text.py, a standard-library HTMLParser
that preserves MathML alttext; it performs no mathematical evaluation.
The three pdftotext attempts returned exit127, `pdftotext: command not found`.
No installation was attempted. Source HTML retrieval and extraction succeeded.
Matlas was called once for the active Silver/joint-potential mechanism; its
generic constant-step hits were not used. Exact recommended primary papers
were then read. Their statements, definitions, and applicable proofs, rather
than search snippets or numerical tables, supplied the source mapping above.

The primary source's recurrence was never executed. No mathematical program,
numerical evaluation, arithmetic replay, solver, benchmark DP, formal gate,
consult, subagent, or installation was used. The algebra above is textual
reasoning. No external theorem or draft in this report has been promoted to a
fact; no unverified component is an accepted predecessor. This complete joint
draft is a finding, with accepted inputs identified in Section1.

Exactly three continuation laws were screened. The one allocated analytical
round is complete; no further allocation or automatic renewal is inferred.
No publication, finalization, revocation, shutdown, or Goal completion action
was taken.

# max66: a complete-prefix objective multiplier law and its exact scoped failure

Status: COMPLETE JOINT ANALYTICAL DRAFT, UNVERIFIED. No formal submission,
no new fact, and no unrestricted objective upper bound are asserted.
The original exact-profile minimax problem remains OPEN.

Outcome of this draft: on the unchanged tau=1/4 words, the symmetric
kernel fails the full PSD test for every m>=3. Its sole backward-tilt
repair, including every nonnegative parameter choice in the displayed
family, fails at the Huber level for every m>=4. In fact any PSD level
within this same law would have to exceed 1/100, so this law cannot even
provide a vanishing objective bound on that sequence. These are scoped,
unverified statements about a proposed certificate family, not W itself.

One mechanism is tested: a product kernel on every directed queried-prefix
pair, completed by explicit minimizer and terminal multipliers. The base
kernel is symmetric. The ONLY repair increases every backward multiplier
by a second product kernel. Both the concrete positive rule and the whole
nonnegative two-parameter family are addressed. This is a whole-word
objective calculation, not a primitive-capacity assumption or a modular
boundary inequality.

## 1. Exact object, accepted inputs, and notation

For a fixed enclosing integer m>=1, put

 lambda_s=1+sqrt(2), beta_word=1-1/(4m),
 c=lambda_s(1+1/(4m)), a=beta_word/c,
 H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j), 0<=j<m,
 h=H_m, n=2^m-1.

Every child in these definitions uses this SAME enclosing m. In particular,
the left word H_(m-1) is not the standalone word with enclosing m-1.
The letter n always denotes the number of actual physical updates. The
terminal gradient is an interpolation variable and supplies no extra update.

The accepted mathematical inputs are:

* fact1252b8823bb5a290: original smooth-convex interpolation, the full
  formal-vector objective certificate convention, and the existence of a
  selected certificate without incoming terminal rows. Its complete proof
  was read. No queried-prefix row deletion follows from that theorem.
* fact04219554e41d9750: the ORIGINAL all-split benchmark recurrence and its
  stated denominator enclosures. Only its unconditional benchmark is used
  in Section 9. No conditional seed substitution or new composition is used.

Normalize L=1, x_star=g_star=f_star=0. The independent formal vectors are

                       x_0,g_0,...,g_n.

Define x_i=x_0-sum_(t<i) h_t g_t, and use the accepted convention

 Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2, i!=j.

All original directed pairs, including the minimizer and terminal, remain
part of the model. A zero multiplier is an explicit choice for this candidate,
not a claim that its row is generally redundant. The certificate polynomial is

 M_cert=q_cert||x_0||^2-2f_n+sum_(i!=j) lambda_ij Q_ij.       (1)

If every multiplier is nonnegative, all value coefficients cancel, and
M_cert is PSD on this entire space, then W_n(h)<=q_cert. This is the
W=2R normalization with the original specified-minimizer distance. The
contrapositive about a PARTICULAR polynomial is not a lower bound on W.

To avoid collisions with earlier project notation, the new objects below
have suffix66. For an arbitrary nonnegative word of length n, let

 S66=sum_(i<n)h_i, D66=1+2S66,
 p66_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, 0<=i<=n,
 T66=sum_(i<n)h_i*p66_i,
 d66_i=sum_(j<i)h_j*(p66_i-p66_j), 0<=i<n.                 (2)

T66=(S66^2-sum h_i^2)/2. Indeed, both sides sum h_i*h_j over j<i.
Thus T66>0 exactly when at least two steps are positive. Sums below are
finite mathematical formulas; none was evaluated by a program.

## 2. One explicit complete-prefix kernel, with its sole repair

Suppose first that S66>0. For real u66,v66>=0, put, for distinct i,j<n,

 K66_ij=h_i*h_j*(u66+v66*1_(i>j)), K66_ii=0,
 r66_i=sum_(j<n)K66_ij-sum_(j<n)K66_ji
       =v66*h_i*(2p66_i+h_i-S66),
 mu66_i=h_i*[2+u66*(S66*p66_i-T66)+v66*d66_i]
              /(2S66-p66_i),
 ell66_i=mu66_i+r66_i, 0<=i<n,
 mu66_n=2-sum_(i<n)mu66_i.                               (3)

The denominator 2S66-p66_i is at least S66>0, including at zero steps.
Specify EVERY original multiplier as follows:

 lambda_ij=K66_ij for distinct i,j<n;
 lambda_(i,star)=mu66_i for 0<=i<=n;
 lambda_(n,i)=ell66_i for 0<=i<n;
 lambda_(star,i)=0 for 0<=i<=n;
 lambda_(i,n)=0 for 0<=i<n;
 lambda_ii=0.                                           (4)

The base is v66=0. The sole repair is v66>0, which retains every forward
prefix pair and adds mass on every backward prefix pair. These are dense
arrays, not thresholded supports extracted from numerical data. The
choice of no incoming terminal rows is compatible with accepted1252, but
the product form (3) is a NEW RESTRICTION of this candidate only.

Here is a completely explicit, strictly sign-valid choice, so (3) is not
just a feasible-set restatement. If T66>0, let b66 be the smallest positive
step and set

 u66=1/T66,
 mu66_i^0=h_i*(1+S66*p66_i/T66)/(2S66-p66_i),
 mu66_n^0=2-sum_(i<n)mu66_i^0,
 J66=sum_(i<n)h_i*d66_i/(2S66-p66_i),
 v66=min{1/[4S66(S66-b66)], mu66_n^0/(2J66)}.             (5)

The base candidate uses the same u66 and v66=0. Formula (5), without
numerical fitting or optimization, is the one repaired candidate.

All divisions and signs in (5) are valid. For h_i>0, p66_i<S66 and
mu66_i^0>0. Directly from (2),

 sum_(i<n)(2S66-p66_i)*mu66_i^0
    =sum_(i<n)h_i*(1+S66*p66_i/T66)=2S66.                (6)

Because every positive term has 2S66-p66_i>S66, (6) gives
sum mu66_i^0<2, so mu66_n^0>0. At the second positive step d66_i>0,
which gives J66>0. Two positive steps also give S66-b66>0. Consequently
v66>0. Moreover

 mu66_i=mu66_i^0+v66*h_i*d66_i/(2S66-p66_i)>=0,
 mu66_n=mu66_n^0-v66*J66>=mu66_n^0/2>0.                 (7)

For a positive h_i, the definition of mu66_i^0 gives
mu66_i/h_i>=1/(2S66). Since h_i>=b66 and p66_i>=0,

 ell66_i/h_i>=1/(2S66)-v66*(S66-b66)>=1/(4S66)>0.       (8)

At h_i=0, mu66_i=ell66_i=0 and every K66 edge incident to i is zero.
All K66 edges between positive steps are strictly positive in BOTH
directions. Thus every coefficient of (4), including the terminal and
minimizer coefficients, has its stated exact sign. The unrepaired v66=0
choice is also nonnegative, with ell66_i=mu66_i^0 and mu66_n=mu66_n^0.

## 3. Complete flow cancellation and the exact Huber-null equations

For i<n, the full outgoing-minus-incoming flow of (4) is

 mu66_i+sum_j K66_ij-sum_j K66_ji-ell66_i=0.             (9)

Every finite prefix edge contributes once to each of its row/column sums,
so sum r66_i=0. At n the outgoing flow is

 mu66_n+sum_(i<n)ell66_i=2,                             (10)

and no edge enters n. At star, the outgoing flow is zero and the incoming
flow is sum_(i<=n)mu66_i=2. Therefore (1) has zero coefficient on every
formal f_i, including f_n. This is the full original value identity.

The finite-prefix drift of the proposed kernel is exactly

 sum_(j<n)K66_ij*(p66_i-p66_j)
  =u66*h_i*(S66*p66_i-T66)+v66*h_i*d66_i.                (11)

Equations (3) and (11) give

 (2S66-p66_i)*mu66_i
      =2h_i+sum_(j<n)K66_ij*(p66_i-p66_j), i<n.         (12)

We also check the terminal equation rather than assuming it. Multiply
r66_i by p66_i and sum to obtain

 sum_i p66_i*r66_i=sum_(i,j<n)K66_ij*(p66_i-p66_j).

Summing (12) consequently yields

 sum_i(2S66-p66_i)*mu66_i=2S66+sum_i p66_i*r66_i.

Using sum r66_i=0 and ell66_i=mu66_i+r66_i then gives

 sum_(i<n)ell66_i*(S66-p66_i)=S66*mu66_n.               (13)

Together (12)--(13) are all the finite-node Huber-null balance equations.
They were derived here; the unverified finding0fda is provenance, not a
premise. The equations by themselves DO NOT imply PSD.

## 4. Full original matrix, including all cross-block entries

Let C66 be the symmetric (n+1)-gradient coefficient matrix of (1). The
complete full matrix is

              M66(q_cert)=[[q_cert,-mu66^T/2],
                           [-mu66/2,C66]],             (14)

where mu66=(mu66_0,...,mu66_n). This is a matrix on the original n+2
coordinates; it is not a matrix restricted to realizable trajectories.

For clarity we derive its entries from ALL original rows. A term
lambda_ij*||g_i-g_j||^2/2 contributes half the total incident mass to
the diagonal and minus lambda_ij to the corresponding cross coefficient.
Only a forward edge out of i contributes -h_i*lambda_ij to ||g_i||^2
through <g_i,x_j-x_i>. By (9)--(10) the half incident mass is
out_i-1_(i=n). Thus, with h_n=0,

 C66_ii=out_i-1_(i=n)-h_i*sum_(j>i)lambda_ij.            (15)

For 0<=i<j<=n, the difference-square terms contribute
-(lambda_ij+lambda_ji). A term based at i with destination after j
contributes -h_j*lambda_ik. A term based at j with destination at or
before i, or at star, contributes h_i times its multiplier. Therefore

 2C66_ij=-(lambda_ij+lambda_ji)
          -h_j*sum_(k>j)lambda_ik
          +h_i*(mu66_j+sum_(k<=i)lambda_jk).             (16)

All sums in (15)--(16) except the explicitly separated star term are
over finite sample indices. Substituting (3)--(4) gives every entry:

 C66_ii=mu66_i+h_i*[u66*(S66-h_i)+v66*p66_i]
                 -u66*h_i^2*(S66-p66_i-h_i), i<n;
 C66_nn=1;                                            (17)

 2C66_ij=h_i*mu66_j+h_i*h_j*[-(2u66+v66)
                 -u66*(S66-p66_j-h_j)
                 +(u66+v66)*(p66_i+h_i)], i<j<n;       (18)

 2C66_(i,n)=-ell66_i
             +h_i*(mu66_n+sum_(t<=i)ell66_t), i<n.     (19)

These expressions include every left/right cross-block coefficient and
every terminal gradient coordinate. No product of child remainders or
independent-gradient assumption has replaced them. The candidate is formed
on the whole parent word; no child slack was assumed zero or discarded.

Here is also a coordinate-free expansion checking the same whole matrix.
Write z_i=-sum_(t<i)h_t*g_t, so x_i=x_0+z_i and z_star=0. The value-free
polynomial is exactly

 q_cert||x_0||^2-sum_(i<=n)mu66_i*<g_i,x_0>
 -sum_(i<=n)mu66_i*<g_i,z_i>+sum_(i<=n)mu66_i*||g_i||^2/2
 +sum_(i<n)ell66_i*[<g_n,z_i-z_n>+||g_n-g_i||^2/2]
 +sum_(i!=j<n)K66_ij*[<g_i,z_j-z_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2].   (20)

In particular, both the entries and their linking to the full matrix are
exact polynomial identities; no numerical matrix is substituted.

Let e66 be the all-ones vector of length n+1. Multiplying the gradient
part of (20) by e66 gives the row identity

 2C66*e66=2h+mu66+drift66,
 drift66_i=p66_i*mu66_i
                +sum_(j finite)lambda_ij*(p66_i-p66_j). (21)

For a direct verification, the squared differences contribute mu66/2
to C66*e66. The bilinear position terms contribute half their row drift
and half the vector sum of their displacements. The latter sum is
-2z_n=2sum h_t*g_t, because full finite flow is 2 at n and all star
outgoing multipliers vanish. This gives (21) on every coordinate.
Equations (12)--(13) now imply

              C66*e66=D66*mu66/2.                     (22)

Consequently M66(1/D66)*(1,e66/D66)=0 exactly. More generally, the
Schur completion in the initial-point coordinate is

 M_cert=q_cert*||x_0-sum_i mu66_i*g_i/(2q_cert)||^2
      +sum_(i,j)[C66_ij-mu66_i*mu66_j/(4q_cert)]<g_i,g_j>,
                                                        (23)

when q_cert>0. At q_cert=1/D66 this Schur remainder annihilates e66,
but will have a strictly negative diagonal in Section 7.

The accepted terminal-square parameterization is respected as well:
alpha_star=mu66_n/2, alpha_i=ell66_i/2. Equations (9)--(10) show that
these sum to one, and their prefix flow is 2alpha_i. Hence the FULL
identity from fact1252 applies with exactly these coefficients, without
any deleted prefix direction. Nothing here assumes its remainder PSD.

## 5. Exact geometry of the unchanged hierarchy

For m>=3, let

 k66=2^(m-1)-1, r66= h_(k66)=1+a*c^(m-1),
 A66=sum H_(m-1), Jleft66=sum_(i<k66)h_i^2,
 E66=A66*r66-Jleft66, Kroot66=3A66+2r66.                (24)

r66 is the physical root step, not r66_i of (3). The whole word is
palindromic, with left/right sums A66, so

 S66=2A66+r66, p66_(k66)=A66,
 T66=2A66^2+2A66*r66-Jleft66,
 d66_(k66)=(A66^2+Jleft66)/2,
 S66*A66-T66=-E66,
 T66-E66=A66*S66.                                     (25)

The identity for d66_(k66) follows by writing
sum_(i<k66)h_i*p66_i=(A66^2-Jleft66)/2 within the actual left block.
Every child step is strictly smaller than r66, because c>1 and it has a
strictly smaller level. Thus 0<Jleft66<A66*r66 and E66>0.

We will need A66>=r66. This is proved inside each enclosing m. Let
s_j=sum H_j and t_j=1+a*c^j. Then

 s_(j+1)=2s_j+t_j, t_(j+1)=c*t_j-(c-1).

For m>=3, 2<c<3 and 0<beta_word<1. Since

 s_2=3+2a+beta_word, t_2=1+beta_word*c,
 s_2-t_2=2+beta_word*(1+2/c-c)>0,                       (26)

the base s_2>t_2 holds. To see the sign without a decimal, c<3 gives
1+2/c-c>-2; multiplication by beta_word in (0,1) keeps the product
strictly above -2, whether that parenthesis is positive or negative.
If s_j>=t_j, then s_(j+1)>=3t_j>c*t_j-(c-1)=t_(j+1).
Induction through j=m-1 gives A66=s_(m-1)>t_(m-1)=r66.

Here c<3 follows from c<=13lambda_s/12 and lambda_s<5/2; the latter is
sqrt(2)<3/2. Also beta_word>=11/12 and lambda_s>12/5, so r66>3 for
m>=3. For m>=4 the simpler estimate

 r66=1+beta_word*c^(m-2)
       >=1+(3/4)*lambda_s^(m-2)>4                     (27)

holds. These are symbolic inductions/inequalities, not executed recurrences.
The first step h_0=1+a is a smallest positive step and is at most A66.

## 6. First-query nonnegativity constrains the entire repaired family

We now allow ANY u66,v66>=0 in (3), not just the explicit choice (5).
Suppose all of (4) are nonnegative. Only one necessary sign, ell66_0>=0,
already imposes a strong condition. Put b=h_0>0 for this section. Since
p66_0=d66_0=0,

 ell66_0=b*[(2-u66*T66)/(2S66)-v66*(S66-b)]>=0.

Therefore

 u66*T66+2S66*v66*(S66-b)<=2,
 u66<=2/T66, v66<=1/[S66(S66-b)].                      (28)

If (28) is violated, an original terminal multiplier is negative, which
already disqualifies that parameter choice. The remaining argument grants
every other sign and still excludes PSD in the stated range.

At the root, (3), (17), and (25) give exact scalar coefficients

 mu66_(k66)=r66*[2-u66*E66+v66*d66_(k66)]/Kroot66,
 C66_(k66,k66)=mu66_(k66)
                   +r66*A66*[u66*(2-r66)+v66].        (29)

Let mu_r denote mu66_(k66) only in the rest of this proof. Because E66>0,
d66_(k66)>0, and (28) holds, (25) gives

 mu_r>=2r66*(1-E66/T66)/Kroot66
      =2r66*A66*S66/(Kroot66*T66)
      >=r66*S66/[Kroot66*(A66+r66)]>0.                (30)

The last inequality uses T66<=2A66*(A66+r66), since Jleft66>=0.

For r66>2, the u66 term in the root diagonal of (29) is nonpositive.
Using (28), (30), and b<=A66 gives

 [v66*r66*A66]/mu_r
 <= A66*Kroot66*(A66+r66)/[S66^2*(S66-b)]
 <= A66*Kroot66/S66^2
 < 3/4.                                               (31)

The last strict sign is exactly

 3S66^2-4A66*Kroot66=4A66*r66+3r66^2>0.

Thus the COMPLETE original root gradient diagonal satisfies

                 C66_(k66,k66)<(7/4)*mu_r.             (32)

On the other hand, A66>=r66 implies

 Kroot66*(A66+r66)<=(10/9)*S66^2,                       (33)

because 10S66^2-9Kroot66*(A66+r66)
=(A66-r66)*(13A66+8r66)>=0. From D66>2S66, (30), and (33),

                     D66*mu_r>9r66/5.                (34)

No step in (28)--(34) assumes PSD or a certificate on an isolated child.
These are necessary constraints for the explicitly defined WHOLE-WORD
kernel with exact nonnegative original multipliers.

## 7. A negative direction in the entire original space

Let q_cert=chi66/D66 with chi66>0. On the two ORIGINAL formal coordinates
(x_0,g_(k66)), the restriction of (14) is precisely

        [[q_cert,-mu_r/2],[-mu_r/2,C66_(k66,k66)]].     (35)

To link this to the full space, take an arbitrary unit Euclidean vector e
and assign

 g_(k66)=e, g_i=0 for every other i including i=n,
 x_0=mu_r*e/(2q_cert).                                (36)

This is an explicit linear embedding of the two-coordinate space into the
original n+2 coordinates. All positions still use their original GD
substitution. Evaluating (20), or completing the square in (35), gives

 M_cert=C66_(k66,k66)-mu_r^2/(4q_cert)
       < (mu_r/4)*[7-9r66/(5chi66)].                  (37)

Hence the full matrix is NOT PSD whenever chi66<=9r66/35. In particular,
for EVERY enclosing m>=4, (27) gives 9r66/35>36/35>1, so the exact Huber
level chi66=1 fails. Its explicit negative value obeys

                    M_cert<-mu_r/20<0.                (38)

This failure applies to the strictly positive rule (5), the symmetric
base, and every other nonnegative u66,v66 completion in this family.
If some other coefficient fails its sign first, that choice is already
excluded before the PSD test. The conclusion is not based on a numerical
eigenvalue or a removed block.

For the base v66=0, (29) gives the stronger bound C66_(k66,k66)<=mu_r.
Equations (34)--(36) therefore exclude the exact Huber level already at
EVERY m>=3, since 9r66/5>27/5>4. The repaired-family assertion above
starts at m=4; no assertion of its PSD feasibility or failure at m=2,3
is required or inferred.

Assignments (36) are formal quadratic-form tests. They are NOT claimed
to be interpolation-feasible transcripts or actual convex functions.
Their role is legitimate because a full dual remainder must be PSD on
ALL original formal vectors. They do not give a lower witness for W.

Any PSD member at a positive level must instead have

                     chi66>9r66/35.                  (39)

If q_cert<=0, the nonzero off-diagonal -mu_r/2 in (35) makes PSD
impossible too. Thus no nonpositive-level exception is being omitted.
Since r66 tends to infinity by (27), (39) excludes every bounded
multiplicative loss relative to 1/D66 within this same multiplier law.
Changing u66,v66 with m or with the chosen level does not evade (28)--(39).
Changing the formulas for mu66 or terminal weights is outside this law.

## 8. Zero physical steps and the small degenerate cases

The target words themselves have every h_i>1. No physical bridge vanishes
at any claimed m. Nevertheless the coefficient definitions have a complete
zero-step interpretation, which is useful for detecting hidden coordinate
identifications.

If S66>0 and T66>0, formulas (2)--(5) apply even with zero steps. Every
zero-step index i<n has h_i=mu66_i=ell66_i=0 and no incident K66 edge.
Equations (17)--(19) make its entire gradient row and column zero.
For nonzero indices, the same formulas depend only on their actual
chronological prefix masses. Thus, after selecting the original indices
of positive steps and the ORIGINAL terminal n, the full matrix is exactly
T_pick^T*Mhat66*T_pick, where T_pick is the coordinate selection map and
Mhat66 uses the compressed positive word. This is a full-space congruence
with zero omitted columns. No equality between formal duplicate gradients
is imposed. This is also the explicit lifting convention in accepted1252.

If S66=0, including n=0, set only lambda_(n,star)=2 and q_cert=1.
Then M_cert=||x_0-g_n||^2 on the entire original space. Every unused
gradient coordinate is a zero column.

If S66>0 but T66=0, exactly one physical step is positive, say h_i=b.
Use u66=v66=0 in (3). The only nonzero multipliers are
lambda_(i,star)=lambda_(n,star)=lambda_(n,i)=1. With D66=2b+1,
the full completion is

 M_cert=(1/D66)||x_0-(D66/2)(g_i+g_n)||^2
                     +(3-2b)||g_i-g_n||^2/4.          (40)

All other original coordinates are free zero columns. Formula (40) is
PSD at the Huber level exactly when 0<b<=3/2; for b>3/2 its difference
direction is negative. This finite calibration is not the claimed
unbounded bridge. For m=1 the target b=1+3/(5lambda_s)<3/2, so the
one-step calibration succeeds. The formula (5) is never divided by zero
in these degenerate cases.

## 9. A stronger limitation on this law, and benchmark orientation

The necessary loss in (39) is not merely a small excess above one.
For m>=4, a=(4m-1)/[(4m+1)lambda_s]>=15/(17lambda_s)>1/3,
because lambda_s<5/2 and 6/17>1/3. For j=m-1, counting the actual
occurrences of each hierarchical step gives

 A66=2^j-1+a*(c^j-2^j)/(c-2).                          (41)

This identity also follows by substitution into s_(j+1)=2s_j+1+a*c^j,
with s_0=0; it is not an evaluated recurrence. Since c>lambda_s>2,
1/(c-2)<=1/(lambda_s-2)=lambda_s<5/2 and 2^j<=c^j. Hence

 A66< c^j+a*c^j/(c-2)
       =a*c^j*[1/a+1/(c-2)]<(11/2)*r66.

It follows that S66<12r66 and, because r66>4,

                       D66<97r66/4.                  (42)

Combining (39) with (42), any member of this law that is actually PSD
at any level necessarily has

 q_cert=chi66/D66>9r66/(35D66)>36/3395>1/100.           (43)

Equation (43) is a LOWER RESTRICTION ON CERTIFICATE LEVELS IN THIS FAMILY.
It is NOT a lower bound on W_n(h), and does not assert that a feasible
member at such a larger level exists.

For completeness, the original benchmark in fact042 is
B_0=C_0=1 and B_n=max_(k+l=n-1)G(C_k,B_l), where
G(A,D)=2A+D/2+sqrt(D^2+8AD)/2. Its split k=0 gives
B_n>=G(1,B_(n-1))>=B_(n-1)+2. Induction yields B_n>=2n+1.
Therefore a feasible upper level from this law would obey

 q_cert/U_n^ZJ=B_n*q_cert>(2n+1)/100 -> infinity
                                      for n=2^m-1.    (44)

In particular it cannot establish a fixed relative improvement below
the original U_n^ZJ at unbounded true horizons. No new benchmark DP or
asymptotic numerical value has been used. There is no actual full-class
upper estimate to combine with the newly accepted stagewise-family
comparison, and the NEGATIVE alternative of PROBLEM.md is not proved.

## 10. Source/data provenance and precise limits

Assignment: TASK.md, completed baseline65, one round through ceiling66,
attempted ceiling67. Initial guidance404a833640d56440, later guidance
b038406fdc2969cd explicitly retaining this assignment. Plan finding
3fe1a6b901887c36. The task allows one analytic repair and no formal gate.

The complete previous discovery ad24079f80cd6a3a was read from
research/small_tau_full_pep_20261001/final_handoff.md. Its existing public
raw files H3/raw.json, H4/raw.json, H5/raw.json, H6/raw.json were opened.
The complete H3 original primal Gram, values, pair labels/multipliers and
dual matrix were read; larger cases were inspected through original first,
terminal, and root rows, saved values/diagnostics and directed multiplier
rows. Those are data reads, not regenerated calculations. Some initial
broad output was truncated; decisive root/sign/model passages were then
reread in bounded output. No claim of inspecting every entry of the larger
arrays is made.

The observed stored root multipliers are markedly directional and vary
among earlier queries. This motivated one backward-tilt repair of a dense
kernel. It did NOT justify equal ratios across backward pairs, or any
exact zero, rank, positivity or PSD assertion. Equations (3)--(5) are
explicit analytic candidate restrictions and are not represented as an
exact reconstruction of the raw arrays. The stored original primal and
dual spectra have small negative eigenvalues. The old six-job allocation
is CLOSED; no old diagnostic was recomputed and no numerical evidence is
a premise of Sections 2--9.

The complete full-certificate finding0fda962c46583d52 and the historical
terminal-reselection draft792aa4b6dbbad8bf were read. Only the latter's
accepted extension1252 is a correctness input. The old four-fan finding
2105aae5913d003a was recalled for its exact restricted scope; the present
law is not a new name for a child endpoint-fan gluing. The boundary result
2f7305e3a99f9978 was read for its contracts and scope: it excludes a
particular modular recharge inequality and expressly leaves whole-parent
certificates open. Neither result is an assumed impossibility premise.

Primary-source retrieval used the search-math-results skill with stage
active_program, mode repair, intent proof-method background, and missing
mechanism full original multiplier signs plus all-depth PSD. Matlas
returned no directly useful theorem for the query. Web retrieval located
Jason M. Altschuler and Pablo A. Parrilo, "Acceleration by Stepsize
Hedging II: Silver Stepsize Schedule for Smooth Convex Optimization,"
arXiv:2309.16530 (2023), Sections 2--3, Theorem 3.4 and Appendix A.2:
https://arxiv.org/pdf/2309.16530 . The exact PDF was downloaded. Its
definitions, proof structure, helper lemmas and deferred quadratic-form
proof were read through the web PDF text and saved as two local text
extracts. The local pdftotext command was unavailable (exit127), and the
immediately following read of the missing text file returned exit2. The
web HTML v4 request had returned404. No package was installed.

The source uses Q^AP_ij=-2Q_ji in our convention and proves a recursive
certificate for h_t=1+lambda_s^(valuation_2(t+1)-1). Its exact Pell mass
and bridge identities differ from our enclosing-m-dependent word. The
method suggests retaining complete interpolation terms before checking
small matrix restrictions; its silver-word theorem is not imported as an
upper bound for this different schedule. No external theorem or computer
algebra script is used as a premise of the obstruction. In particular no
Matlas result is asserted applicable and no external_refs submission occurs.

The result is confined to formulas (3)--(4), u66,v66>=0, with their
Huber-null minimizer/terminal completion. It does NOT exclude pair- or
level-dependent kernels, different completions at higher objective levels,
other terminal selections, hierarchical corrections, general full duals,
or the existence of a Huber-tight upper certificate for these words.
It supplies no non-Huber lower witness, and no lower bound on unrestricted
W or on the minimax value. It does not invoke the stagewise construction
as an upper estimate. The original question remains OPEN.

# max67: an asymmetric exact-copy transport and its full-objective obstruction

Status: complete joint analytical draft, UNVERIFIED. There is no new fact,
formal submission, unrestricted objective upper bound, or actual lower
witness. The original minimax problem remains OPEN.

This round tests one specific asymmetric transport. It copies the right
child's finite interpolation coefficients, scales its optimum attachments
separately, and permits arbitrary position-dependent corrections throughout
the left block. Its sole repair adds forward cross-block mass and makes the
backward transport affine in the right child's chronological position.

The outcome is a full-original-space PSD obstruction, not just a failed
bridge inequality. For every actual enclosing m>=1536, every sign-valid,
value-cancelling completion in the specified family would require

                  q_cert*(1+2 sum H_m) > 11/10.                 (0)

The same conclusion covers the unrepaired rule. With the original benchmark,
any sequence of PSD levels within this family would satisfy

       liminf_m B_(2^m-1)*q_cert,m > 3619/3600 > 1.             (1)

Thus this particular rule cannot supply the required improvement at
unbounded true horizons. It does not exclude different terminal destinations,
a different right optimum scale, corrections to the right finite block,
non-affine transport, optimum-outgoing coefficients at a larger objective
level, or general asymmetric full certificates.

## 1. Exact physical words, verified inputs, and the viability requirement

Fix one enclosing integer m. Throughout the entire hierarchy use

 lambda_s=1+sqrt(2), beta_word=1-1/(4m),
 c=lambda_s*(1+1/(4m)), a=beta_word/c,
 H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j), 0<=j<m.

Every occurrence of a child uses these SAME parameters. The number of
physical updates in H_m is n_m=2^m-1. The terminal gradient is a formal
interpolation variable; it supplies no extra update.

The accepted mathematical inputs are fact1252b8823bb5a290, for the original
objective cone; fact04219554e41d9750, for the unconditional original benchmark
and its certified interval table; and fact5fd396eba75651e7, for the stated
Huber/benchmark comparison on these exact words. No conditional seed clause
of fact042 is used. The two numerical/analytical findings242f8d453ac26183
and dd4045b39aa0c257 are provenance and awareness, not correctness premises.

Before attempting a recursive PSD proof, a prospective whole-word level
q_cert=chi/(1+2 sum H_m) must be checked at the original B_n scale. The
accepted inequality B_(n_m)/(1+2 sum H_m)<237/250 would suffice with a
uniform chi<250/237. Multiplying a fixed loss greater than one at every
depth does not meet this condition. For example (101/100)^m>=1+m/100,
so an assertion of a one-percent loss at each level cannot be replaced
by an assertion of a one-percent whole-word loss. The obstruction below
is stronger: even the loss needed at the final join is too large at the
actual limiting B_n scale.

All new calculations in this document are symbolic textual derivations.
No schedule, recurrence, matrix, spectrum, fitted coefficient, or benchmark
has been evaluated by a mathematical program in this round.

## 2. Original cone and an exact full matrix formula

Normalize L=1 and x_star=g_star=f_star=0. For independent formal vectors
(x_0,g_0,...,g_n), define x_i=x_0-sum_(t<i)h_t*g_t. All distinct ordered
original interpolation rows remain in the model:

 Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2.

Let lambda_ij be their nonnegative multipliers. Values cancel in

 M(q)=q||x_0||^2-2f_n+sum_(i!=j)lambda_ij*Q_ij               (2)

exactly when out_i-in_i=2*1_(i=n) at every finite i. A valid upper
certificate requires PSD on the ENTIRE original formal space, as specified
in fact1252. In the tested family lambda_(star,i)=0. Write
nu_i=lambda_(i,star), and z_i=-sum_(t<i)h_t*g_t. Once flow cancels values,
the complete quadratic, with every original gradient retained, is

 M(q)=q||x_0||^2-sum_i nu_i<g_i,x_0>
      +sum_i nu_i[-<g_i,z_i>+||g_i||^2/2]
      +sum_(i!=j finite)lambda_ij
           [<g_i,z_j-z_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2].                 (3)

Thus its full matrix is [[q,-nu^T/2],[-nu/2,C]], where C acts on
all n+1 finite gradient coordinates. If h_n=0, its entries are

 C_ii=out_i-1_(i=n)-h_i*sum_(j>i)lambda_ij,
 2C_ij=-(lambda_ij+lambda_ji)-h_j*sum_(v>j)lambda_iv
          +h_i*(nu_j+sum_(v<=i)lambda_jv), i<j.             (4)

The sums in (4) are over finite labels; out_i includes nu_i. To verify
the diagonal, the squared gradient differences contribute half the incident
mass, which is out_i-1_(i=n) by flow. A forward position term based at i
contributes -h_i*lambda_ij. For i<j, squared differences contribute
-(lambda_ij+lambda_ji) to the cross coefficient. Position terms based at i
with destination after j contribute -h_j times their multipliers; those
based at j with destination at or before i, including the optimum,
contribute h_i times their multipliers. This proves (3)-(4) directly.

Zeros assigned below are restrictions on this candidate's coefficients,
not assertions that any original interpolation row is redundant.

## 3. One asymmetric transport; exactly one affine repair

All quantities introduced in this section are local to the displayed join;
in particular its gradient matrix C is distinct from the benchmark C_n.
Consider a parent (H_j,d,H_j), where k=2^j-1, j>=1, and d=1+a*c^j.
Let the child steps be u_0,...,u_(k-1), with

 p_i=sum_(v<i)u_v, 0<=i<=k, A=p_k>0,
 t=A+d, S=2A+d, D=1+2S, D_child=1+2A.

Left finite labels are L_l=l, 0<=l<=k; L_k is the physical bridge
query. Right finite labels are R_i=k+1+i, 0<=i<=k. The actual parent
terminal is R_k=2k+1. Their chronological masses are p_l and t+p_i.

Take ANY nonnegative reference finite array K_ij (i!=j, 0<=i,j<=k), K_ii=0,
and nonnegative optimum attachment vector mu_i satisfying

 sum_j K_ij-sum_j K_ji+mu_i=2*1_(i=k).                    (5)

Summing (5) gives sum_i mu_i=2. No PSD assumption, objective bound,
rank, or child oracle is used in the obstruction. Incoming child-terminal
coefficients in K may be present. In particular the ensuing argument is
stronger than one limited to already certified children.

Set

 theta=(3A+d)/(2S), s=1-theta=(A+d)/(2S).                 (6)

The right finite block is an EXACT copy and its optimum attachments have
the separate scale theta:

 lambda_(R_i,R_v)=K_iv, lambda_(R_i,star)=theta*mu_i.     (7)

This exact-copy condition and (6) are new candidate restrictions, not
conclusions drawn from the floating-point fits.

The base rule is kappa=0. Its ONLY repair is

                         kappa=s/(2d).                  (8)

For either choice, define the complete backward transport by

 lambda_(R_i,L_0)=mu_i*[s-kappa*d*(A-p_i)/A],
 lambda_(R_i,L_k)=mu_i*kappa*t*(A-p_i)/A,
 lambda_(R_i,L_l)=0 for 0<l<k.                           (9)

Retain the forward direction, with arbitrary nonnegative multipliers
lambda_(L_l,R_i) whose column totals are

 F_i=sum_l lambda_(L_l,R_i)=kappa*mu_i*(A-p_i).           (10)

Both directions are therefore explicitly present in the repaired rule.
If mu_i>0 and i<k, F_i>0; the repaired backward coefficient to L_0 is
strictly positive too. At the original terminal, p_k=A, so F_k=0,
lambda_(R_k,L_k)=0 and lambda_(R_k,L_0)=s*mu_k.

ALL left finite rows lambda_(L_l,L_v), and ALL left optimum attachments
nu_(L_l), may be chosen independently of any scalar child-copy law,
with arbitrary dependence on position, m and q. The obstruction grants
every nonnegative such choice for which full original flow holds. This
includes adding nonzero incoming edges at the bridge query L_k. There
is no assumption that it remains an unqueried child terminal.

Here is an explicit full nonnegative flow completion, showing that these
prescribed rows are not merely a hypothetical feasible-set description.
Put

 E=sum_i mu_i*(A-p_i), 0<=E<=2A.

Send every forward F_i from L_k; set all left finite pairs to zero; set

 nu_(L_0)=2s-kappa*d*E/A, nu_(L_k)=kappa*d*E/A,
 nu_(L_l)=0 for 0<l<k.                                  (11)

Every optimum-outgoing coefficient is zero. Together (7),(9)-(11) specify
every original multiplier. The theorem below also covers all the more
general left corrections and forward distributions just described.

### Exact signs and value cancellation

The inequalities 0<theta<1, s>0, d>0 and 0<=p_i<=A imply all the claimed
signs. In the repaired rule,

 s-kappa*d*(A-p_i)/A=s*(A+p_i)/(2A)>=s/2.

In (11), 0<=kappa*d*E/A<=s, so nu_(L_0)>=s>0 and nu_(L_k)>=0.
The base has nu_(L_0)=2s, nu_(L_k)=0. All other coefficients are
nonnegative multiples of mu_i or entries of K, or are explicitly zero.

The backward outgoing total at R_i is

 B_i=mu_i*[s+kappa*(A-p_i)]=s*mu_i+F_i.                 (12)

By (5), the right value flow is
(2*1_(i=k)-mu_i)+theta*mu_i+B_i-F_i=2*1_(i=k).
At L_0, the incoming backward sum equals the first quantity in (11).
At L_k, incoming backward minus outgoing forward is
kappa*(t/A-1)*E=kappa*d*E/A, equal to its optimum attachment.
All other left flows vanish in the explicit completion. Thus every
finite f_i cancels in (2), including f_n; the total optimum incoming
mass is 2. No interpolation row or value variable is silently omitted.

### Why these coefficients were tested

The base routes the right terminal's backward correction to the actual
initial left sample. If an input child has exact Huber contact and mu_k>0,
its terminal row equation is sum_v K_kv*(A-p_v)=A*mu_k.
For the parent, that row at exact Huber contact would require
S*theta*mu_k=A*mu_k+S*s*mu_k. Solving gives (6). Thus theta is derived
from this chosen terminal attachment in the original identity. It is
not a fitted numerical constant.

The base's right-node flow permits no forward mass. The sole repair
allows such mass to increase linearly with the remaining right mass
A-p_i and distributes backward mass between the original endpoints
L_0,L_k. Formula (9) has the required mass (12) and first moment
kappa*t*(A-p_i), which interpolates the two endpoint contact conditions.
Its interior contact defect is calculated explicitly in Section 9.
The largest kappa allowed by the displayed L_0 sign is s/d; (8) uses
half of this exact cap to leave that multiplier strictly positive.
This is one analytic repair, not a scan over coefficient choices.

The initial-point terminal destination is a substantive restriction of
the tested law. It need not match the numerical optimizers. In particular
this draft does not claim that all asymmetric recursions must use (6).

## 4. Feasible starting arrays and the recursion's actual scope

For H_0, the only nonzero multiplier lambda_(0,star)=2 at q=1 gives
M=||x_0-g_0||^2. For the actual H_1=(b), b=1+a, use

 lambda_(0,star)=lambda_(1,star)=lambda_(1,0)=1,

with every other original coefficient zero. Direct expansion gives

 M(1/(1+2b))
   =1/(1+2b)*||x_0-(1+2b)(g_0+g_1)/2||^2
       +(3-2b)||g_0-g_1||^2/4.                         (13)

For the physical family, a<1/lambda_s=sqrt(2)-1, so
1<b<sqrt(2)<3/2; (13) is PSD on all three formal coordinates.
This base is part of the joint derivation, not a separately promoted fact.

Equations (5),(7),(9)-(11) define an actual nonnegative value-flow
recursion from this base for every later child, always with the enclosing
m fixed. They do NOT define a PSD recursion: that is precisely what the
full-space test below refutes. No new stronger child invariant is presumed,
and no virtual child oracle or primitive-capacity result replaces a parent
calculation. The full proof of failure even grants arbitrary arrays (5)
and arbitrary sign-valid left corrections at the final join.

## 5. The first full-space PSD obstruction

Evaluate the entire original quadratic on the following original-coordinate
linear subspace, in one real dimension:

 g_(L_l)=0 for every 0<=l<=k,
 g_(R_i)=v for every 0<=i<=k,
 x_0=x.                                                   (14)

The terminal gradient remains present and equals v in this TEST subspace;
no equality constraint has been imposed on the certificate. A PSD matrix
must remain nonnegative on every such subspace. The original substitutions
give x_(L_l)=x and x_(R_i)=x-p_i*v, including the terminal.
These formal test vectors are not asserted to satisfy interpolation or to
be an actual convex-function trajectory; they test the full dual matrix.

Every left finite quadratic and left optimum quadratic vanishes on (14),
irrespective of how large or position-dependent its coefficient is. The
right finite block contributes

 sum_(i,l)K_il*(p_i-p_l)*v^2
       =[2A-sum_i mu_i*p_i]*v^2,                         (15)

where (15) is just (5) multiplied by p_i and summed, not a use of
child PSD or an independent-gradient assumption about actual iterates.
The right optimum rows contribute
-2theta*x*v+theta*sum_i mu_i*(p_i+1/2)*v^2.
The backward rows contribute sum_i B_i*(p_i+1/2)*v^2;
the forward rows contribute sum_i F_i*v^2/2. Inserting (12) proves
the exact whole-parent identity

 M(q)|_(14)=q*x^2-2theta*x*v+Gamma*v^2,
 Gamma=1+2A+kappa*sum_i mu_i*(A-p_i)*(1+p_i).             (16)

In particular (16) is linked explicitly to ALL original rows and to the
original (n+2)-coordinate matrix (3)-(4). It is not an autonomous bridge
inequality, a relaxed child's bound, or a virtual matrix of lower order.

For q>0, set v=1 and x=theta/q. This is an explicit full-space direction,
whose quadratic value is

                         Gamma-theta^2/q.              (17)

If q<0, x alone already gives a negative direction. If q=0, the nonzero
coefficient -2theta*x*v in (16) does. Hence any PSD level in this law
is positive and necessarily satisfies q>=theta^2/Gamma.

## 6. A loss larger than 11/10, including the forward repair

For 0<=p<=A, completing a scalar square gives

 (A-p)(1+p)=(A+1)^2/4-[p-(A-1)/2]^2<=(A+1)^2/4.

Since sum mu_i=2, the repaired Gamma satisfies

 Gamma<=1+2A+s*(A+1)^2/(4d)=:Gamma_up.                   (18)

The unrepaired Gamma=1+2A is no larger, so it is enough to treat (18).
Suppose

                    A>=1024, 2/5<=r=d/A<=1/2.          (19)

Then theta=(3+r)/(2(2+r)), s=(1+r)/(2(2+r))<=3/10 and

 Gamma_up=A*[2+s/(4r)]+1+s/(2r)+s/(4rA).

The coefficient of A in 10D*theta^2-11Gamma_up is

 P(r)/[8r(2+r)],
 P(r)=40r^3+64r^2-3r-11.

For r=2/5+w with w>=0, direct symbolic expansion is

 P(r)=3/5+(337/5)w+112w^2+40w^3>=3/5.

Also 8r(2+r)<=10. Therefore

 10D*theta^2-11Gamma_up
   >=3A/50+10theta^2-11[1+s/(2r)+s/(4rA)]>0.           (20)

For the last strict inequality, s/(2r)<=3/8,
s/(4rA)<=3/(16*1024), so the bracket is less than 3/2;
3A/50>=3*1024/50>33/2. Every inequality is exact.

It follows that D*theta^2>(11/10)*Gamma. Consequently for every
chi=qD<=11/10 with q>0, (17) is strictly negative. This proves (0)
under (19). It applies even if all unspecified left multipliers and the
forward distribution are allowed to depend arbitrarily on q and m.
Such choices cannot change (16). If their signs or value flow fail,
they are already disqualified before this PSD test.

This is a restriction on certificate LEVELS IN THIS LAW. It is neither
a lower bound on unrestricted W nor an assertion that the law becomes
PSD at any larger level.

## 7. The unchanged physical hierarchy satisfies (19)

For the actual final join let j=m-1. Counting the occurrences of each
physical step, or proving the same identity by symbolic induction, gives

 A=2^j-1+a*(c^j-2^j)/(c-2), d=1+a*c^j.                 (21)

For m>=1536, put u=1/(4m). Since sqrt(2)<10/7, u<=1/256 gives
c<(17/7)(257/256)=4369/1792<49/20<5/2. Since sqrt(2)>7/5,
c>12/5. Also beta_word>=3/4, hence a>3/10.

Write z=(2/c)^j. Because c>12/5, z<(5/6)^j. The inequality
(5/6)^4=625/1296<1/2 and j>=24 give z<1/64.
Using (21), d>a*c^j, and 1/(c-2)<1/(lambda_s-2)=lambda_s,

 A/d < 1/(c-2)+z/a
       <17/7+5/96<5/2.                                (22)

Conversely, dropping the positive term 2^j-1 from (21) gives

 A>(d-1)*(1-z)/(c-2)
      >(20/9)*(63/64)*(d-1)=(35/16)*(d-1)>2d.          (23)

Here d>12 follows from a>3/10, c>2 and j>=6. Equations (22)-(23)
give 2/5<d/A<1/2. Finally every physical child step exceeds one, so
A>2^j-1>=2^11-1>1024. This proves (19) for EVERY m>=1536 without
replacing a child by a newly parameterized hierarchy or executing a
recurrence. Therefore (0) holds at the required true horizons.

## 8. Comparison with the ORIGINAL benchmark

The accepted unconditional ray theorem in fact042 states

 Z_1=lim_m lambda_s^(-m)*B_(2^m-1),
 Z_1>=lambda_s^(-j)*B_(2^j-1) for every j>=0.

Section 8 of that fact certifies every interval in the public fixed table
research/audit_c78/input_interval_table.csv, whose SHA256 is
2a7585bc0bade9f85461456c13d60bbe618f85c7cc816e1ddc5b4c1a1d611e68.
The row 1023 was read in this round, not recomputed. With

 D80=1208925819614629174706176,
 L1023=19205851554343006584447327614,

the accepted interval gives D80*B_1023>=L1023>15850*D80.
For an explicit integer comparison, the last difference is
44377313451134165354438014>0. Also
lambda_s^10=3363+2378sqrt(2)<6727, since sqrt(2)<99/70.
Thus the displayed accepted enclosure and elementary comparisons give

 Z_1>15850/6727>47/20,                                 (24)

where 20*15850-47*6727=831>0. No new DP, interval generation, numerical
root, arithmetic replay, or certificate checker was run.

For completeness, the exponential series and k!>=2*3^(k-2), k>=2,
give

 exp(1/4)<1+1/4+(1/32)/(1-1/12)=113/88<9/7.            (25)

There is strictness because the factorial inequality is strict from k=4.
Accepted fact5fd gives, for the unchanged tau=1/4 physical words,

 B_(n_m)/(1+2 sum H_m) -> Z_1/[2exp(1/4)]>329/360.     (26)

If there were a sequence of full PSD levels q_cert,m in the tested
family, (0) and (26) would imply

 liminf_m B_(n_m)*q_cert,m
    >=(11/10)*Z_1/[2exp(1/4)]>3619/3600>1.

The same statement holds along any unbounded subsequence of such levels.
This proves (1) and rules out a NEGATIVE-alternative certificate through
this law. It does not lower-bound W_(n_m)(H_m): a different multiplier
family could certify a smaller value.

## 9. Why an affine transport also loses exact Huber contact

This additional identity diagnoses the repair, without being a premise
of Sections 5-8. Suppose the reference child really has Huber contact
and mu_k>0. A Huber-tight full parent must also have contact. Indeed
take Hub(x)=x^2/2 for |x|<=1 and Hub(x)=|x|-1/2 otherwise, and its
actual trajectory from x_0=D. Every x_i=D-P_i>=S+1>1, so all finite
g_i=1 and f_i=x_i-1/2. Its terminal numerator 2f_n is D and its initial
square is D^2;
every finite Q_ij and every Q_(i,star) is zero, and
Q_(star,i)=1-x_i<0. Nonnegative multipliers and PSD at q=1/D force
optimum-outgoing multipliers to vanish and M(D,1,...,1)=0. A PSD
quadratic with zero value at a vector annihilates that vector: apply
it to that vector plus an arbitrary scalar multiple of another vector.

Writing P_i for original chronological mass, expansion of (3) gives
the finite-coordinate contact equations

 (2S-P_i)*nu_i=2h_i+sum_(v finite)lambda_iv*(P_i-P_v).  (27)

More explicitly, if e is the all-ones gradient vector, differentiating
(3) at equal gradients gives
2(Ce)_i=2h_i+(1+P_i)*nu_i+sum_v lambda_iv*(P_i-P_v).
In the position terms, full finite value flow makes the sum of the
position-derivative contributions 2h_i; the remaining derivative based
at i is its displayed outgoing drift. The norm terms contribute nu_i.
The gradient part of M(D,e)=0 is 2Ce=D*nu, which yields (27).

For the right finite copy (7), the child version of (27) subtracts out.
Let backward coefficients more generally have the form
lambda_(R_i,L_l)=mu_i*(b_l+c_l*p_i), with every actual coefficient
nonnegative. Permit arbitrary nonnegative forward coefficients, and
require their terminal column to be zero. Put
B0=sum b_l, B1=sum c_l, M0=sum p_l*b_l, M1=sum p_l*c_l.
Right flow gives F_i/mu_i=B0+B1*p_i-s at every mu_i>0.
The right equations (27) require

 (t+p_i)*(B0+B1*p_i)-(M0+M1*p_i)
       =theta*t-s*(2A-p_i).                            (28)

If there are at least three distinct positions with mu_i>0, equality
of the two degree-at-most-two polynomials at those positions forces
B1=0. At the terminal, zero forward inflow and mu_k>0 then give
B0=s. Thus every F_i=0. At mu_i=0, flow and the backward formula
also force F_i=0. Nonnegativity makes EVERY forward coefficient zero.

Consequently an affine backward source profile cannot retain genuine
forward mass while simultaneously preserving this exact finite copy,
a single right attachment scale, and Huber contact. This is scoped to
the stated law; it does not follow from the approximate numerical fits.

For the explicit repair (9), the backward first moment is
kappa*t*(A-p_i). At the endpoints p_i=0,A the intended right equation
is matched. At an interior position its drift exceeds the required drift
by exactly kappa*mu_i*p_i*(A-p_i). This provides the concrete interior
contact defect. The result of Sections 5-8 is stronger for the selected
theta,kappa: raising q within a small whole-word budget cannot repair
the full PSD failure, even if the contact requirement is abandoned.

# Goal PAUSED by operator on 2026-10-08; original target OPEN

---
fact_id: 5e1848ee0b153f5f
problem_id: gd-exact-profile-20260926
author: max3
predecessors: [04219554e41d9750, 4c92d3fc4eb6153e]
glossary_introduces:
  C_pc: Positive certified upper bound on Z_d/Z_(b_pc0) for the chosen positive integer anchor d.
  F_pc_i(t): Certified ratio upper bound U_pc(m*b_pc_i,t)/(a*L_pc(b_pc_i,t)) at nonnegative integer selection stage t.
  H_(i,j): Actual substituted schedule Hsub_(n_(i,j)) of length N_(i,j).
  J: In this phase-cover theorem, the least nonnegative integer with (3/2)^J>=60*b_pc_max/gamma, equal to the displayed logarithmic ceiling.
  J5: Explicit common output depth ceil(log(600000*6^16)/log(3/2)) for the seventeen-phase length-five corollary.
  J5a: Explicit common output depth ceil(log(360000*6^8)/log(3/2)) for the nine-phase anchored length-five corollary.
  J6: Explicit common output depth ceil(log(1200000*7^10)/log(3/2)) for the eleven-phase length-six corollary.
  L_pc(b,j): Certified lower ray endpoint lambda^(-j) B_(b*2^j-1), for positive integer b and nonnegative integer j.
  N_(i,j): Actual physical length m*n_(i,j)=m*b_pc_i*2^j-m.
  P_pc: Finite product upper bound lambda^ell*C_pc.
  U_pc(b,j): Certified upper ray endpoint lambda^(-j)(sqrt(B_(b*2^j))+3/100)^2.
  b_pc0: Positive integer anchor base for the finite multiplicative phase cover.
  b_pc_i: Integer phase base b_pc0*m^i for 0<=i<=K, where m=r+1.
  b_pc_i_star: The phase base b_pc_i evaluated at the index i_star supplied by the finite product argument.
  b_pc_max: Largest candidate phase base b_pc_(K-1)=b_pc0*m^(K-1).
  d_shift: Nonnegative integer offset in the shifted-ray estimate, independent of the positive integer anchor d.
  i_star: A fixed index in {0,...,K-1} whose normalized ratio is at most 1-gamma; certified selection can return another index with ratio below 1-gamma/2.
  n_(i,j): Input tree node count b_pc_i*2^j-1 for phase i and nonnegative integer output depth j.
  q_pc_i(a): Normalized ray ratio Z_(m*b_pc_i)/(a*Z_(b_pc_i)), where a is the locally defined capacity floor.
  q_pc_i_star(a): The normalized ratio q_pc_i(a) evaluated at the fixed index i_star.
  t_pc: Normalized product bound P_pc/a^K in (0,1).
  u_j: Auxiliary error majorant lambda^(-j/2) at nonnegative integer output depth j.
external_refs: []
